Figure 5
.

An illustration of the interaction between threads in
\{{t_{\alpha}^{x}},{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{t_{x}},t^{\prime}_{x}\}
, demonstrating no-skipping on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
.
We denote by
*
an unspecified value in an event, which corresponds to a value specific to the guessed solution of the PCP instance.

Fig.

5
illustrates the interaction between the threads
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}
,
{t_{x}}
and
t^{\prime}_{x}
, which forces
{t_{x}}
to not skip any of the writes of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
, and thus read the whole solution to the PCP instance guessed by
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
.
Next, we explain how this pattern is achieved.
Events that are not part of this pattern are omitted for readability.

Synchronization between
{t_{x}}
and
t^{\prime}_{x}
.

The locations
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, synchronize
{t_{x}}
and
t^{\prime}_{x}
.
As shown in
Fig.

5
,
{t_{x}}
and
t^{\prime}_{x}
alternate reads and writes on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
, forming the same pattern that was used for the synchronization between the guesser threads in
Fig.

4
.
The first field of the values assures that each
\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
reads from
t^{\prime}_{x}
and
\mathtt{r}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
reads from
{t_{x}}
.
These reads follow a pattern identical to the synchronization between the guesser threads, establishing the invariant

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})

The same pattern holds between
{t_{x}}
and
t^{\prime}_{x}
over locations
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, establishing also that

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})

Synchronization between
{t_{y}}
and
{t^{\prime}_{y}}
.

Similarly to above, the locations
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, synchronize
{t_{y}}
and
{t^{\prime}_{y}}
.
The pattern between
{t_{y}}
and
{t^{\prime}_{y}}
over locations
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
, and the pattern between
{t_{y}}
and
{t^{\prime}_{y}}
over locations
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, establish the invariants

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}})

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})

No-skipping between guesser and verifier threads.

We are finally ready to show how the no-skipping property holds on locations
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, between guesser and verifier threads.

No-skipping on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
.

We illustrate how
{t_{x}}
is forced to not skip any of the writes of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
, by exploiting the interactions between
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}
,
{t_{x}}
, and
t^{\prime}_{x}
, shown in
Fig.

5
.
First, we have that
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
(edge
1
), forced by writing/reading a unique value (the uniqueness is established by the overline in
\overline{{t_{\alpha}^{x}}}
, which is not used again).
Since we also have
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
(per the
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-order of
{t_{x}}
), this implies that
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
(edge
2
), due to
.
Due to
Section

3.1
, we have that
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}})
(edge
A
), which implies that
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
for all
i\geq 2
.
If
\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
for any
i\geq 2
, then we would have
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
.
Since, by
Section

3.1
, we have
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
(edge
B
), together with
\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
(edge
2
), this would create a violation of
.
We thus have
\mathtt{w}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
(edge
3
).
Since we also have
\mathtt{w}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
(per the
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-order of
t^{\prime}_{x}
), this implies that
\mathtt{w}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
(edge
4
), due to
.
Due to
Section

3.1
, we have
\mathtt{w}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x}})
(edge
C
), which implies that
\mathtt{w}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
for all
i\geq 3
.
If
\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{2}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
for any
i\geq 3
, then we would have
\mathtt{w}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
.
Since, by
Section

3.1
, we have
\mathtt{w}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{1}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
(edge
D
), together with
\mathtt{w}_{1}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{1}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
, this would create a violation of
.
We thus have
\mathtt{w}_{2}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{2}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
(edge
5
), i.e., the second read of
{t_{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
cannot skip the second write of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
, as desired.
The process repeats inductively to establish the no-skipping invariant

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})

Finally, notice that we have not created any
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}
paths from
{t_{x}}
or
t^{\prime}_{x}
to any of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
or
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}
, and thus the two pairs of threads can execute asynchronously.

No-skipping on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
.

The pattern of interaction between
{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{x}}
,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
,
{t_{x}}
and
t^{\prime}_{x}
is essentially identical to the above case, and can be obtained from
Fig.

5
by replacing every occurrence of
\alpha
by
\beta
(e.g.,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
instead of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
).
This ensures that
{t_{x}}
does not skip any of the writes of
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, i.e.,

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})

No-skipping on
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
.

Finally, similar patterns force
{t_{y}}
to not skip any of the writes on
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
(by
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
) and
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
(by
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
).
The reasoning is nearly identical to the above, and can be obtained by replacing every occurrence of
x
by
y
.
This establishes the no-skipping invariants

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}})

\forall i\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\quad\text{and}\quad\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})

3.2.
The Formal Reduction

We now present the formal reduction.
For clarity of exposition, we use common pseudocode language to represent the concurrent program.
Though we do not explicitly define a semantics for this language, its translation to an LTS is intuitive and straightforward (see, e.g.,
(
17
)
).

Thread
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}

Thread
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}

Thread
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}

Thread
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}

⬇

\beta_{\bot}\leftarrow\bot
;

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\leftarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\textsc{NDet}(\{1,\dots,n\})}
;

while

\mathsf{T}

do

prev\leftarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt}
;

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt}\leftarrow({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt}+1)\bmod 4
;

if

First()

then

\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}},\langle\overline{{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
;

\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}},\langle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
;

\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt})
;

else

\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}},\langle{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
;

\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}},\langle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
;

\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt})
;

\mathtt{r}({\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x,y}},prev)
;

\mathtt{r}({\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},prev)
;

endif

if

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}=\bot

then

break
;

endif

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\leftarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\textsc{NDet}(\{1,\dots,n,\bot\})}
;

done

\mathsf{term}

\mathsf{term}

Figure 6
.

The program
\mathsf{P}
corresponding to a PCP instance
\mathcal{I}
.

Reduction.

Given a PCP instance
\mathcal{I}
, we construct the program
\mathsf{P}
shown in
Fig.

6
.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\textsc{NDet}(X)}
refers to a non-deterministic choice of an element of a set
X
,
while a call to
First()
in a line returns
\mathsf{T}
(true) the first time this particular line is executed, and
\mathsf{F}
(false) in every subsequent call.
Each counter
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt}
is initialized to
0
, and so is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}cnt_{y}}
when present.
Every location
u
is either written by only one thread, or all writes to
u
in thread
t
are of the form
\mathtt{w}(u,\langle t,v\rangle)
and all reads from
u
in thread
t^{\prime}
are of the form
\mathtt{r}(u,\langle t,v\rangle)
, where the
t
indicates the thread this event must to read from.
The program follows the overview presented in
Section

3.1
and
Figs.

3
,
4
and
5
.

First, consider the threads
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
and
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
.
The main loop of each thread is over the value of its respective thread-local location
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
, which contains a non-deterministic guess of the index
i\in\{1,\dots,n\}
of the string
\alpha_{i}
to be appended to the PCP sequence, or
\bot
, which marks the end of guessing.
In order to avoid producing an empty sequence, the first assignment to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
cannot be
\bot
.
Thread
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
communicates the guessed value to
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
through
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{\alpha}}}
, and thread
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
checks, at the end of its while loop, that it matches its own value.
The location
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{\alpha}}}
, alongside
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x,y}}
, is also used to ensure synchronization between
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
and
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
, by creating the pattern of interaction shown in
Fig.

4
.

Thread
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
communicates its guess in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
to thread
{t_{y}}
by writing its value on
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
.
In particular,
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
performs a write
\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}},\langle\overline{{t_{\alpha}^{y}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
in the first iteration, and a write
\mathtt{w}({\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}},\langle{t_{\alpha}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
in all subsequent iterations.
The purpose of using
\overline{{t_{\alpha}^{y}}}
instead of
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
is to distinguish the first write, and not allow the first read on
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
on
{t_{y}}
to skip reading from it.
These writes on
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
are also interleaved with other writes and reads on
{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{y}}
, which create a pattern of interaction with
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}}
following
Fig.

4
.

Thread
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
also contains a for loop, inside its while loop, in which it writes the characters of
\alpha_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}}
, one-by-one, to
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
, to be read by
{t_{x}}
.
Similarly to the write to
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
by
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
, the first write to
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
by
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
is specially signaled by using
\langle\overline{{t_{\alpha}^{x}}},\alpha_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}}[1]\rangle
instead of
\langle{t_{\alpha}^{x}},\alpha_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}}[1]\rangle
as its value.
These writes are interleaved with other reads and writes on
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}}
, which create a pattern of interaction with
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}
following
Fig.

4
.
This pattern and its interactions with writes to
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
is also shown in
Fig.

5
.

Threads
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
and
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
follow a symmetric process as
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
and
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
, which can be explicitly defined by replacing all occurrences of
\alpha
to
\beta
above.

Next, consider the thread
{t_{x}}
.
Its main loop is over the value of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
, which contains a non-deterministic guess of the
i
-th letter of the PCP string.
It later performs the reads
\mathtt{r}({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}},\langle\overline{{t_{\alpha}^{x}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
and
\mathtt{r}({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}},\langle\overline{{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
in its first iteration, and the reads
\mathtt{r}({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}},\langle{t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
and
\mathtt{r}({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}},\langle{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}\rangle)
in subsequent iterations, verifying that the
i
-th letter produced by
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
and the
i
-th letter produced by
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
match.
This thread also writes to locations
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, and reads from location
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
in order to create a pattern of interaction with
t^{\prime}_{x}
following
Fig.

5
.
Thread
{t_{y}}
is symmetric to
{t_{x}}
, but guesses indexes of the PCP solution instead of letters and uses the locations
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
, instead of
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
.

Finally, the auxiliary threads
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{x}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}}
,
t^{\prime}_{x}
and
{t^{\prime}_{y}}
perform a non-deterministic sequence of writes and reads of values not related to the PCP instance.
This sequence establishes a synchronous pattern of interaction with the main threads and force the verifier threads
{t_{x}}
and
{t_{y}}
to not skip any of the writes of the guesser threads
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
,
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
and
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
, as shown in
Fig.

5
.

3.3.
Soundness

We first establish the soundness of our reduction, as stated in the following lemma.

Lemma 3.1.

If
\mathsf{P}
has an execution where all threads reach
\mathsf{term}
then
\mathcal{I}
has a solution.

The proof is based on the following no-skipping lemma, which establishes that for every thread
t
and location
u
, the
i
-th read of
t
on
u
can only read from the
i
-th write of the (unique) thread writing the corresponding value to
u
.

Lemma 3.2.

Consider any execution
G=(E,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo})
of
\mathsf{P}
where all threads reach
\mathsf{term}
.
For all
i
such that the corresponding read event
\mathtt{r}_{i}
exist in
G
, we have the following
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}
-edges:

\displaystyle\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-7.65005pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{{z^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-8.50012pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{y}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{y}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-4.25006pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-6.37509pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}).

Eq.

15
establishes the synchronization of guesser threads; its proof follows the steps outlined in the paragraph
Guesser threads
of
Section

3.1
.

Eq.

16
establishes the synchronization of verifier threads; its proof follows the same pattern as that of
Eq.

15
, as discussed in the paragraph
Verifier threads
of
Section

3.1
.
Finally,
Eq.

17
establishes no-skipping between guesser and verifier threads; its proof is more involved, and follows the general steps outlined in the paragraph
No-skipping between guesser and verifier threads
of
Section

3.1
.

Proof of
Lemma

3.1
.

We finally argue that if all threads in
\mathsf{P}
reach
\mathsf{term}
, then
\mathcal{I}
has a solution.

Let
j_{1},\dots,j_{k},\bot
be the sequence of values guessed on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
in
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
.
Since, by
Eq.

15
of
Lemma

3.2
, we have
\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{\alpha}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{\alpha}}})
, this must also be the sequence of values guessed on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
in
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
.
Notice that
j_{i}\neq\bot
for all
i\in[k]
, which is enforced by the looping condition on
while
on
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
and
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
.
Moreover, the initial assignment of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
enforces
k\geq 1
.
The second field of the values written to
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
by
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
thus forms the sequence
j_{1},\dots,j_{k},\bot
, and the second field of the values written to
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
by
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
forms the word
\alpha_{j_{1}}\cdots\alpha_{j_{k}}\cdot\bot
.
Similarly, let
j^{\prime}_{1},\dots,j^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}},\bot
be the sequence of values guessed on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
in
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
and
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
.
The interaction of
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
and
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}}
ensures this is indeed the same sequence.
The second fields of the values written to
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
by
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
form the sequence
j^{\prime}_{1},\dots,j^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}},\bot
, and the second fields of the values written to
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
by
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
form the word
\beta_{j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdots\beta_{j^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}}}\cdot\bot
.

Let
\mathsf{id}_{1},\dots,\mathsf{id}_{m},\bot
be the sequence of values guessed on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
in
{t_{y}}
.
By construction, this is the sequence of values that
{t_{y}}
reads from
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
and
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
.
By
Eq.

17
of
Lemma

3.2
,
\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})
and
\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})
for all
i\in[m]
, and since the values read correspond to
\mathsf{id}_{1},\dots,\mathsf{id}_{m},\bot
, we have that
\mathsf{id}_{1},\dots,\mathsf{id}_{m}=j_{1},\dots,j_{k}=j^{\prime}_{1},\dots,j^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}}
.
The final value
\bot
forces
k=k^{\prime}=m
, i.e., that the sequence of values written by
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
and
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
is read fully.
A similar argument holds for
{t_{x}}
, yielding that the sequence
\mathsf{lt}_{1},\dots,\mathsf{lt}_{\ell},\bot
assumed by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
in
{t_{x}}
is such that
\mathsf{lt}_{1}\cdots\mathsf{lt}_{\ell}=\alpha_{j_{1}}\cdots\alpha_{j_{k}}=\beta_{j^{\prime}_{1}}\cdots\beta_{j^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}}}
.
We thus have that
j_{1},\dots,j_{k}
is a solution to the PCP instance
\mathcal{I}
.

3.4.
Completeness

Finally, we establish the completeness of the reduction: if
\mathcal{I}
has a solution then all threads in
\mathsf{P}
can reach
\mathsf{term}
simultaneously.

Lemma 3.3.

If
\mathcal{I}
has a solution then
\mathsf{P}
has an execution where all threads reach
\mathsf{term}
.

Given a solution to
\mathcal{I}
, we construct an execution graph
G
where every thread behaves according to
\mathsf{P}
, and then prove that
G
is consistent under Release/Acquire.
This direction is considerably more intricate compared to soundness: whereas the no-skipping lemma for soundness (
Lemma

3.2
) follows by analyzing the interaction of small sets of threads (each of size either
2
or
4
), proving the consistency of
G
has to account for all threads simultaneously.

The execution graph
G=\langle E,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}\rangle
.

Let
j_{1},\dots,j_{k}
be a solution to
\mathcal{I}
; we construct an execution graph
G=\langle E,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}\rangle
of
\mathsf{P}
in which all threads reach
\mathsf{term}
.

The events of
G
.

We specify the events of
G
by listing how each thread
behaves according to the solution
j_{1},\dots,j_{k}
of
\mathcal{I}
.
The event sequence of the non-primed threads
{t_{\alpha}^{x}},{t_{\alpha}^{y}},{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}},{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},{t_{x}}
, and
{t_{y}}
is defined by the values assumed by their respective locations
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
, since this resolves all non-determinism locally in each thread.
For the threads
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
,
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}

{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
, and
{t_{y}}
, we assign to the respective locations
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}aux}
the sequence of values
j_{1},\dots,j_{k},\bot
, while for
{t_{x}}
, we assign the sequence of letters in
\alpha_{j_{1}}\cdots\alpha_{j_{k}}\cdot\bot
.
Thus,
{t_{\alpha}^{y}}
(resp.,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}}
) writes
j_{1},\dots,j_{k},\bot
(one-by-one, in this order) to
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}
(resp.,
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
), and
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
(resp.,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
) writes
\alpha_{j_{1}}\cdots\alpha_{j_{k}}\cdot\bot
(letter-by-letter, in this order) to
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
(resp.,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
) in its innermost loop.
Threads
{t_{x}}
and
{t_{y}}
read the respective values, one-by-one, in each iteration.

The event sequence of the primed threads
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}},{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{x}},{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}},t^{\prime}_{x},
and
{t^{\prime}_{y}}
, is defined by how many times their while loops execute.
The threads
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{x}}
, and
{t_{x}}
execute their loops
|\alpha_{j_{1}}\cdots\alpha_{j_{k}}\cdot\bot|
times, and threads
{{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}}
,
{{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}}
, and
{t_{y}}
executes their loops
k+1
times.

Specifying
G.\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}
.

Recall that we use notation of the form
\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})
to refer to the
i
-th write of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
, and
\mathtt{r}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})
to refer to the
i
-th read of
t^{\prime}_{x}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}
.
Also recall that we use event values to force each read
\mathtt{r}_{i}
to read from a particular thread.
We construct a reads-from relation that specifies
\mathtt{w}_{i}\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}$}}\mathtt{r}_{i}
for all
i
, where
\mathtt{w}_{i}
is the
i
-th write of the unique thread writing a value that matches that of
\mathtt{r}_{i}
.
In particular,
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}
consists exactly of the relations shown in
Eqs.

15
,
16
and
17

of
Lemma

3.2
.
It is straightforward to verify that all the such
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}
relationships are valid in the sense that the values read and written match.

Specifying
G.\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}
.

Next, we specify the
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}
relation of
G
.
First, for any two writes of the form
\mathtt{w}_{i}(t,u)
and
\mathtt{w}_{j}(t^{\prime},u)
(i.e., the
i
-th and the
j
-th write on location
u
of threads
t
and
t^{\prime}
, respectively), if
i<j
then
\mathtt{w}_{i}\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{j}
.
It remains to order concurrent writes with the same index.
We resolve these orderings through the following
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}
-edges.

\displaystyle\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.12503pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-4.25006pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{\alpha}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-6.37509pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}),

(18)

\displaystyle\mathtt{w}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.12503pt\mathtt{w}_{i}(t^{\prime}_{x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-4.25006pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{\alpha}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}}),

\displaystyle\hskip-6.37509pt\mathtt{w}_{i}({t^{\prime}_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.20555pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}$}}\mathtt{w}_{i}({{t^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}^{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}).

Fig.

5
illustrates some of these
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}
-edges.
Note that each of the above locations is written by two threads, while each other location is written by a single thread, hence
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}
is total on each location.

In the remaining of this section, we prove that
G
is consistent under Release/Acquire.
A key notion used extensively in our proofs is monotonicity with respect to event indices, as defined below.

Monotonicity.

Given a binary relation
R
over the events of
G
, we call an
R
-edge
e_{i}\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$R$}}e_{j}

non-decreasing
(resp.,
increasing
) if
i\leq j
(resp.,
i<j
), where
i
and
j
are the indices of
e_{i}
and
e_{j}
, respectively.
We say that
R
is non-decreasing (resp., increasing) if all its edges are non-decreasing (resp., increasing).
Observe that
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}rf}
and
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}mo}
are non-decreasing, by construction.
The crux of our proofs for the consistency of
G
lies in some key monotonicity properties for
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}
that imply the irreflexivity of the relations in
Fig.

1
.
The main challenge is that
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}
is
not
non-decreasing, in general.

Decreasing
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges.

The
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0.4,0}hb}
relation is not non-decreasing because certain
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges of
G
are decreasing.
We identify and characterize two classes of such edges that will require special treatment in our monotonicity lemmas later.
The first class involves edges that can decrease by more than one.
We call the events that receive such edges
bridge events
.
The second class consists of
decreasing read-to-read
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges
between non-bridge events.

Bridge events.

Thread
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
(resp.,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
) writes to
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x,y}}
and reads from
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{\alpha}}}
(resp.,
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x,y}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}}
) less often than it writes/reads other locations.
This is because these locations are only accessed once per iteration of the
outer
loop of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
(resp.,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
), while other locations are accessed once per iteration of the
inner
loop of
{t_{\alpha}^{x}}
(resp.,
{t_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x}}
).
This results in
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges entering the events of the outer loop potentially decreasing arbitrarily.
We call each location among
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{\alpha}^{x,y}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{\alpha}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.76,0.01,0}z_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}^{x,y}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}{\ell_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}}
, a
bridge location
,
and an event
e
accessing a bridge location a
bridge event
.
Bridge events are highlighted in
Fig.

4
.
The next lemma captures some monotonicity properties of
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges that do not involve bridge events.
Its proof follows immediately by examining the sequence of events that each thread executes in isolation.

Lemma 3.4.

Consider any
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edge
e_{i}\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}$}}\mathtt{w}_{j}
where
\mathtt{w}_{j}
is a non-bridge write.
Then

(i)

i\leq j
, and

(ii)

if
\mathsf{op}(e_{i})=\mathtt{r}
, then
i<j
.

Read-to-read edges.

In the first iteration of the while loop in
{t_{x}}
and
{t_{y}}
, read events in certain locations are skipped, while read events in other locations are executed.
This causes the indices of the read events in the skipped locations to lag behind the indices of the other read events by one, thereby causing decreasing read-to-read
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges.
For example, the edge
\mathtt{r}_{3}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}})\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}$}}\mathtt{r}_{2}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})
, in
Fig.

5
, is decreasing because in the first iteration of its while loop,
{t_{x}}
reads from
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}
, but not
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}}
.
The next lemma is an exhaustive account of all decreasing
\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}
-edges between non-bridge read events.
Similarly to
Lemma

3.4
, its proof follows directly by examining the sequence of events that each thread generates in isolation.

Lemma 3.5.

Consider any decreasing edge
\mathtt{r}_{i}\xrightarrow{\raisebox{-1.50694pt}[0.0pt][0.0pt]{\footnotesize$\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}po}$}}\mathtt{r}_{j}
where neither
\mathtt{r}_{i}
nor
\mathtt{r}_{j}
is a bridge event.
Then
j=i-1
, and
\langle\mathtt{r}_{i},\mathtt{r}_{j}\rangle
is one of the following pairs

\displaystyle\langle\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}),\mathtt{r}_{j}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\rangle,\quad\langle\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{\alpha}}),\mathtt{r}_{j}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\rangle

\displaystyle\langle\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}),\mathtt{r}_{j}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\rangle,\quad\langle\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}x_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}}),\mathtt{r}_{j}({t_{x}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}x^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\rangle

\displaystyle\langle\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}),\mathtt{r}_{j}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{\alpha}})\rangle,\quad\langle\mathtt{r}_{i}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{1,0,0.87}y_{\alpha}}),\mathtt{r}_{j}({t_{y}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,1}y^{\prime}_{{\mathchoice{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}{\vbox to4.30554pt{\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\tenrm\beta$}\hss}}\hss}}\vss\par}}}}})\rangle

(19)
